00053: def cube(owner, name, size, center, color):
00054:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=center, color=color))
00055: 
00056: def beam(owner, name, a, b, width, depth, color):
00057:     dx, dz = b[0]-a[0], b[2]-a[2]
00058:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
00059:     n = box((width,depth,length),
00060:             center=((a[0]+b[0])/2,(a[1]+b[1])/2,(a[2]+b[2])/2),
00061:             color=color)
00062:     n.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx,dz)))
00063:     return owner.include(name,n)

00054: def add(name, node):
00055:     return S.include(name, node)
00056: 
00057: def cube(owner, name, size, center, color):
00058:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=center, color=color))
00059: 
00060: def beam(owner, name, a, b, width, depth, color):
00061:     dx, dz = b[0]-a[0], b[2]-a[2]
00062:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
00063:     n = box((width,depth,length),
00064:             center=((a[0]+b[0])/2,(a[1]+b[1])/2,(a[2]+b[2])/2),
00065:             color=color)
00066:     n.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx,dz)))
00067:     return owner.include(name,n)

00338: def textile(name,base,patterned=False):
00339:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00340:     mat.diffuse_color=(*base,1)
00341:     mat.use_nodes=True
00342:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00343:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00344:     shader=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00345:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value=(*base,1)
00346:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.88
00347:     coordinates=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00348:     noise=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00349:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value=180
00350:     noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2
00351:     links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"],noise.inputs["Vector"])
00352:     bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00353:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.12
00354:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.006
00355:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00356:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],shader.inputs["Normal"])
00357: 
00358:     if patterned:
00359:         # Linear coordinate bands avoid the broad, nearly invisible sine ramp.
00360:         separate=nodes.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ")
00361:         links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"],separate.inputs[0])
00362:         multiply=nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00363:         multiply.operation="MULTIPLY"
00364:         multiply.inputs[1].default_value=18
00365:         links.new(separate.outputs["Y"],multiply.inputs[0])
00366:         fraction=nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00367:         fraction.operation="FRACT"
00368:         links.new(multiply.outputs[0],fraction.inputs[0])
00369:         ramp=nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00370:         ramp.color_ramp.interpolation="CONSTANT"
00371:         ramp.color_ramp.elements.remove(ramp.color_ramp.elements[1])
00372:         colors=[
00373:             (0,base),(.07,(.59,.42,.39)),(.105,base),
00374:             (.17,(.66,.55,.45)),(.19,base),
00375:             (.70,(.66,.55,.45)),(.72,base)
00376:         ]
00377:         for index,(position,color) in enumerate(colors):
00378:             element=(ramp.color_ramp.elements[0] if index==0
00379:                      else ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(position))
00380:             element.position=position
00381:             element.color=(*color,1)
00382:         links.new(fraction.outputs[0],ramp.inputs[0])
00383:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],shader.inputs["Base Color"])
00384:     return mat

00407: def mesh_object(name,vertices,faces,material):
00408:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00409:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices,[],faces)
00410:     mesh.update()
00411:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)

00419: vertices=[]
00420: faces=[]
00421: segments=96
00422: rings=32
00423: for j in range(rings+1):
00424:     latitude=-math.pi/2+math.pi*j/rings
00425:     radial=max(0,math.cos(latitude))**.36
00426:     vertical=math.copysign(abs(math.sin(latitude))**.65,
00427:                            math.sin(latitude))
00428:     for i in range(segments):
00429:         angle=2*math.pi*i/segments
00430:         x=math.copysign(abs(math.cos(angle))**.47,math.cos(angle))
00431:         y=math.copysign(abs(math.sin(angle))**.63,math.sin(angle))
00432:         vertices.append((
00433:             .015+(CHAISE_LENGTH/2-.035)*radial*x,
00434:             (CHAISE_WIDTH/2+.002)*radial*y,
00435:             SEAT_HEIGHT+.165*vertical
00436:         ))
00437: for j in range(rings):
00438:     for i in range(segments):
00439:         a=j*segments+i
00440:         b=j*segments+(i+1)%segments
00441:         faces.append((a,b,b+segments,a+segments))

00305: def textile(name, base, patterned=False):
00306:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00307:     mat.diffuse_color = (*base, 1)
00308:     mat.use_nodes = True
00309:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00310:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00311:     shader = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00312:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*base, 1)
00313:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .91
00314: 
00315:     coordinates = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00316:     noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00317:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 155
00318:     noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00319:     links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"], noise.inputs["Vector"])
00320:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00321:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .13
00322:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .012
00323:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00324:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], shader.inputs["Normal"])
00325: 
00326:     if patterned:
00327:         wave = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexWave")
00328:         wave.wave_type = "BANDS"
00329:         wave.bands_direction = "Y"
00330:         wave.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 13
00331:         wave.inputs["Distortion"].default_value = .15
00332:         links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"], wave.inputs["Vector"])
00333:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00334:         colors = [
00335:             (0, base), (.73, base), (.79, (.67, .49, .46)),
00336:             (.83, base), (.92, base), (.955, (.75, .60, .53)),
00337:             (1, base)
00338:         ]
00339:         ramp.color_ramp.elements.remove(ramp.color_ramp.elements[1])
00340:         for index, (position, color) in enumerate(colors):
00341:             element = (ramp.color_ramp.elements[0] if index == 0
00342:                        else ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(position))
00343:             element.position = position
00344:             element.color = (*color, 1)
00345:         links.new(wave.outputs["Color"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
00346:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], shader.inputs["Base Color"])
00347:     return mat
00348: 
00349: cloth = textile("Woven ivory and faded rose stripes", fabric, True)
00350: plain = textile("Cream pillow textile", (.89, .83, .70))
00351: 
00352: # Target SceneAct members through their ancestor markers.
00353: for ob in list(bpy.data.objects):
00354:     if ob.type != "MESH":
00355:         continue
00356:     ancestor = ob
00357:     ancestry = []
00358:     while ancestor is not None:
00359:         ancestry.append(ancestor.name.lower())
00360:         ancestor = ancestor.parent
00361:     if any("pillow" in name for name in ancestry):
00362:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00363:         ob.data.materials.append(plain)
00364:     elif any("far_arm_upholstery" in name for name in ancestry):
00365:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00366:         ob.data.materials.append(cloth)
00367: 
00368: upholstery_group = bpy.data.objects.new("Tailored chaise upholstery", None)
00369: bpy.context.collection.objects.link(upholstery_group)
00370: chaise_marker = next(
00371:     (ob for ob in bpy.data.objects
00372:      if ob.type == "EMPTY" and ob.name.lower() == "chaise"), None
00373: )
00374: if chaise_marker is not None:
00375:     upholstery_group.parent = chaise_marker
00376:     upholstery_group.matrix_parent_inverse = chaise_marker.matrix_world.inverted()
00377: 
00378: def mesh_object(name, vertices, faces, material):
00379:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00380:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices, [], faces)
00381:     mesh.update()
00382:     ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)
00383:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00384:     ob.data.materials.append(material)
00385:     ob.parent = upholstery_group
00386:     ob.matrix_parent_inverse = upholstery_group.matrix_world.inverted()
00387:     for polygon in mesh.polygons:
00388:         polygon.use_smooth = True
00389:     return ob

00391: # Broad, gently domed mattress with rounded corners and a low vertical edge.
00392: vertices = []
00393: faces = []
00394: segments = 96
00395: rings = 32
00396: for j in range(rings+1):
00397:     latitude = -math.pi/2+math.pi*j/rings
00398:     radial = max(0, math.cos(latitude))**.36
00399:     vertical = math.copysign(abs(math.sin(latitude))**.65,
00400:                              math.sin(latitude))
00401:     for i in range(segments):
00402:         angle = 2*math.pi*i/segments
00403:         x = math.copysign(abs(math.cos(angle))**.47, math.cos(angle))
00404:         y = math.copysign(abs(math.sin(angle))**.63, math.sin(angle))
00405:         vertices.append((
00406:             .015 + (CHAISE_LENGTH/2-.035)*radial*x,
00407:             (CHAISE_WIDTH/2+.002)*radial*y,
00408:             SEAT_HEIGHT+.165*vertical
00409:         ))
00410: for j in range(rings):
00411:     for i in range(segments):
00412:         a = j*segments+i
00413:         b = j*segments+(i+1)%segments
00414:         faces.append((a, b, b+segments, a+segments))
00415: mesh_object("Continuous upholstered mattress", vertices, faces, cloth)
